Nemotron-Cascade 2：采用 Cascade RL 和多域按策略蒸馏的训练后法学硕士Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

杨卓林Zhuolin Yang[*][∗], 刘子涵, Zihan Liu []_ , 杨晨, Yang Chen _[] , 戴文亮, Wenliang Dai []_ , 王博新, Boxin Wang _[] , 林胜杰, 李灿奎, 陈杨毅, 蒋东福, 何家凡, Sheng-Chieh Lin, Chankyu Lee, Yangyi Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Jiafan He[‡] , 皮仁杰, Grace Lam, Nayeon Lee, Alexander Bukharin, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Wei Ping, Renjie Pi, Grace Lam, Nayeon Lee, Alexander Bukharin, Mohammad Shoeybi, Bryan Catanzaro, Wei Ping [*][†]

摘要Abstract

我们~~推出 Nemotron-Ca~~ scade ~~2，这是一个开放的 30B MoE 模型，具有 ~~ 3B 激活参数，可提供一流的推理和强大的代理功能。尽管尺寸紧凑，但其数学和编码推理性能接近前沿开放模型。这是继 DeepSeekV3.2-Speciale-671B-A37B 之后第二个实现We ~~introduce Nemotron-Ca~~ scade ~~2, an open 30B MoE m~~ odel w ~~ith 3B activated parame~~ ters th ~~at delivers best-in-~~ class reasoning and strong agentic capabilities. Despite its compact size, its mathematical and coding reasoning performance approaches that of frontier open models. It is the second open-weight LLM, after DeepSeekV3.2-Speciale-671B-A37B, to achieve金奖Gold Medal在2025年国际数学奥林匹克（IMO）、国际信息学奥林匹克（IOI）和ICPC世界总决赛中表现出色，在参数减少20倍的情况下展现出极高的智力密度。与 Nemotron-Cascade 1 相比，关键技术进步如下。在精心策划的数据集上进行 SFT 后，我们大幅扩展了 Cascade RL，以覆盖更广泛的推理和代理领域。此外，我们在整个 Cascade RL 过程中从每个域的最强中间教师模型中引入多域同策略蒸馏，使我们能够有效地恢复基准回归并在此过程中保持强劲的性能提升。我们发布了模型检查点和训练数据的集合。-level performance in the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO), the International Olympiad in Informatics (IOI), and the ICPC World Finals, demonstrating remarkably high intelligence density with 20× fewer parameters. In contrast to Nemotron-Cascade 1, the key technical advancements are as follows. After SFT on a meticulously curated dataset, we substantially expand Cascade RL to cover a much broader spectrum of reasoning and agentic domains. Furthermore, we introduce multi-domain on-policy distillation from the strongest intermediate teacher models for each domain throughout the Cascade RL process, allowing us to efficiently recover benchmark regressions and sustain strong performance gains along the way. We release the collection of model checkpoint and training data.

  • Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B：基于Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B-Base的后训练模型。Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B: the post-trained model based on Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B-Base.

  • Nemotron-Cascade-2-SFT-Data：Nemotron-Cascade-2 的 SFT 数据集集合。Nemotron-Cascade-2-SFT-Data: collection of SFT datasets for Nemotron-Cascade-2.

  • Nemotron-Cascade-2-RL-Data：Nemotron-Cascade-2 的 RL 数据集集合。Nemotron-Cascade-2-RL-Data: collection of RL datasets for Nemotron-Cascade-2.

Nemotron-Cascade 2：采用 Cascade RL 和多域按策略蒸馏的训练后法学硕士Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

一、简介1. Introduction

强化学习（RL）（Guo et al., 2025; Ouyang et al., 2022）已成为法学硕士培训后的基石，推动推理、代理能力和现实世界问题解决的进步。由于模型面临着日益复杂的要求，主要挑战在于成功整合更广泛的 RL 环境以及非常多样化的推理和代理任务。扩展强化学习以涵盖多方面的现实世界应用程序需要强大的框架，能够处理各种奖励信号和复杂的环境反馈，而不会破坏训练过程的稳定性。Reinforcement Learning (RL) (Guo et al., 2025; Ouyang et al., 2022) has emerged as the cornerstone of LLM post-training, driving advances in reasoning, agentic capabilities, and real-world problem-solving. As models are tasked with increasingly sophisticated requirements, the primary challenge lies in successfully incorporating a broader array of RL environments and very diverse reasoning and agentic tasks. Scaling RL to encompass multifaceted, real-world applications necessitates robust frameworks capable of handling varied reward signals and complex environmental feedback without destabilizing the training process.

我们之前的工作 Nemotron-Cascade 1（Wang 等人，2025）引入了 Cascade RL，这是一个跨专业任务领域协调顺序、按领域 RL 训练的框架。 Cascade RL 显着简化了与多域 RL 相关的工程复杂性，同时在各种基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。 Cascade RL 的优点有三个。首先，特定领域的 RL 阶段对Our previous work, Nemotron-Cascade 1 (Wang et al., 2025), introduced Cascade RL, a framework that orchestrates sequential, domain-wise RL training across specialized task domains. Cascade RL significantly simplifies the engineering complexity associated with multi-domain RL while achieving state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of benchmarks. The advantages of Cascade RL are threefold. First, domain-specific RL stages are remarkably resistant to灾难性遗忘catastrophic forgetting。它们很少会降低早期领域获得的基准性能，甚至可能会提高它。其次，它允许针对每个特定领域仔细定制强化学习超参数和训练课程，从而优化学习动态并提高最终表现。第三，每个强化学习阶段内的任务同质性还可以节省大量计算资源，因为域内的响应长度和验证挂钟时间比联合训练的多个域之间更加一致。. They rarely degrade benchmark performance attained in earlier domains and may even improve it. Second, it allows RL hyperparameters and the training curriculum to be carefully tailored to each specific domain, enabling optimized learning dynamics and improved final performance. Third, task homogeneity within each RL stage also yields substantial compute savings, as response lengths and verification wall-clock times are more uniform within a domain than across multiple domains trained jointly.

在这项工作中，我们介绍了 Nemotron-Cascade 2，这是一种具有 3B 激活参数的开放 30B 专家混合 (MoE) 模型。与其前身类似，Nemotron-Cascade 2 进一步在高优先级领域扩展 Cascade RL，以保留领域训练的优势，使我们能够将关键领域的推理性能极限推向最先进的水平。此外，我们将同策略蒸馏（Xiao et al., 2026; Zeng et al., 2026）纳入 Cascade RL 训练阶段。通过在级联强化学习期间从每个特定领域中表现最佳的中级教师模型中提取知识，该机制可以有效地恢复在日益复杂的强化学习环境中训练时可能发生的任何基准回归。此外，我们将多域强化学习集成到级联强化学习中，用于具有相似响应格式和可比验证成本的任务组，使它们能够联合训练，以适应更多强化学习环境，并在跨任务干扰最小的情况下提高训练效率。In this work, we introduce Nemotron-Cascade 2, an open 30B Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 3B activated parameters. Similar to its predecessor, Nemotron-Cascade 2 further scales Cascade RL on high-priority domains to preserve the benefits of domain-wise training, enabling us to push the limits of reasoning performance in key domains to state-of-the-art levels. Furthermore, we incorporate on-policy distillation (Xiao et al., 2026; Zeng et al., 2026) into Cascade RL training stages. By distilling knowledge from the best-performing intermediate teacher models within each specific domain during Cascade RL, this mechanism effectively recovers any benchmark regressions that can occur when training in increasingly complex RL environments. In addition, we integrate multi-domain RL into Cascade RL for groups of tasks with similar response formats and comparable verification costs, allowing them to be trained jointly to scale up for more RL environments and improve training efficiency when cross-task interference is minimal.

我们的 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B 在数学和编码推理方面实现了突破性的性能，尽管只是 30B MoE 模型[1]，但在 2025 年国际数学奥林匹克 (IMO) 和国际信息学奥林匹克 (IOI) 中均获得了金牌，同时还在广泛的基准测试中提供了一流的性能，包括对齐、指令跟踪、长上下文（例如，1M 上下文窗口）和代理任务。完整结果请参见表 1。我们完全开源模型权重、训练数据和方法细节，使研究社区能够重现、分析和扩展所提出的 Cascade RL 训练范例。Our Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B achieves breakthrough performance in mathematical and coding reasoning, securing gold-medal results in both the 2025 International Mathematical Olympiad (IMO) and the International Olympiad in Informatics (IOI) despite being only a 30B MoE model,[1] while also delivering best-in-class performance across a broad range of benchmarks, including alignment, instruction-following, long context (e.g., 1M context window), and agentic tasks. See Table 1 for the full results. We fully open source the model weights, training data, and methodological details, enabling the research community to reproduce, analyze, and extend the proposed Cascade RL training paradigm.

我们将本报告的其余部分整理如下。第 §2 节总结了主要结果。第 §3 节描述了监督微调（SFT）以及数据管理的详细信息。第 §4 节介绍了与多域同策略蒸馏集成的 Cascade RL 框架。第§5节详细介绍了IMO的评估设置和结果，第§6节介绍了IOI和ICPC世界总决赛的评估设置和结果。We organize the remainder of this report as follows. Section §2 summarizes the main results. Section §3 describes the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with details on data curation. Section §4 presents Cascade RL framework intergrated with the multi-domain on-policy distillation. Section §5 details the evaluation setup and results on IMO, while Section §6 presents the evaluation setup and results on IOI and the ICPC World Finals.

The detailed performance of our model is reported in Table 2. It underperforms Qwen3.5-35B-A3B primarily on knowledge-intensive and agentic tasks, highlighting the importance of stronger knowledge-intensive pretraining and agentic RL in future work.

Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B 在几乎所有基准测试中也优于 Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B，尽管这两个模型都是从相同的预训练模型 Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B-Base 进行后训练的（NVIIDA，2025）。这一结果进一步证明了我们的 Cascade RL 加 MOPD 训练流程的有效性。Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B also outperforms Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B on nearly all benchmarks, even though both models are post-trained from the same pretrained model, Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B-Base (NVIIDA, 2025). This result further demonstrates the effectiveness of our Cascade RL plus MOPD training pipeline.

3. 监督微调3. Supervised Fine-Tuning

在本节中，我们将描述监督微调（SFT）的训练框架和数据管理过程，这是我们训练后管道的第一阶段。此阶段为模型配备基础能力，包括推理、对话能力、指令遵循以及代理和软件工程技能。In this section, we describe the training framework and data curation process for supervised fine-tuning (SFT), the first stage of our post-training pipeline. This stage equips the model with foundational capabilities, including reasoning, conversational ability, instruction following, and agentic and software engineering skills.

3.1.培训框架3.1. Training Framework

3.1.1.概述3.1.1. Overview

我们的 SFT 数据涵盖广泛的领域，包括数学、编码、科学、工具使用、代理任务和软件工程，以及更一般的领域，如多轮对话、知识密集型问答、创意写作、角色扮演、安全和指令遵循。Our SFT data spans a broad range of domains, including mathematics, coding, science, tool use, agentic tasks, and software engineering, as well as more general domains such as multi-turn dialogue, knowledge-intensive question answering, creative writing, role-playing, safety, and instruction following.

我们将所有 SFT 样本打包成最多 256K 个令牌的序列，并在一个阶段中训练模型。根据经验，我们发现 SFT 模型在大约 1.5 个 epoch 后达到最佳性能。 SFT 训练超参数可以在附录 B 中找到。We pack all SFT samples into sequences of up to 256K tokens and train the model in a single stage. Empirically, we find that the SFT model reaches optimal performance after approximately 1.5 epochs. The SFT training hyperparameters can be found in Appendix B.

3.1.2.聊天模板3.1.2. Chat Template

我们的聊天模板如图 1 所示。与 NemotronCascade 相比，聊天模板有两处变化（Wang 等人，2025）。首先，为了简单起见，我们删除了 /think 和 /no_think 标签。其次，我们在前面添加一个空的Our chat template is depicted in Figure 1. There are two changes to the chat template compared with NemotronCascade (Wang et al., 2025). First, we remove the /think and /no_think tags for simplicity. Second, we prepend an empty阻止激活非思考模式。block to activate the non-thinking mode.

对于工具调用任务，我们在系统提示符中指定所有可用的工具For tool calling task, we specify all available tools in the system prompt within the和and标签，并指示模型执行包装在tags, and instruct the model to perform tool calls wrapped within the和and标签。tags.

图 1：（左）聊天模板使用相邻Figure 1: (Left) The chat template uses adjacent表示非思考模式的标记，以及单个tokens to indicate non-thinking mode, and a single随后followed by\n来表示思维模式。 （右）对于工具调用，系统提示中列出了可用的工具。该模型被指示调用内的工具to indicate thinking mode. 3.2. SFT 数据管理3.2. SFT Data Curation

3.2.1.数学3.2.1. Math

我们的非证明数学提示主要来源于 Nemotron-Cascade (Wang et al., 2025) 和 NemotronMath-v2 (Du et al., 2025)，我们从中收集了 180 万个工具调用（即 python）样本和 190 万个非工具样本，并由 DeepSeek-V3.2 和 DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale (Liu et al., 2025) 生成响应。 2025），分别。此外，我们从 Nemotron3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025) 的生成选择类别（无工具调用）收集了 676K 样本，并由 GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025) 生成响应。总共，竞赛数学 SFT 包括 180 万个工具调用样本和 260 万个不使用工具的样本。Our non-proof math prompts are primarily sourced from Nemotron-Cascade (Wang et al., 2025) and NemotronMath-v2 (Du et al., 2025), from which we collect 1.8M tool calling (i.e., python) samples and 1.9M non-tool samples, with responses generated by DeepSeek-V3.2 and DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale (Liu et al., 2025), respectively. In addition, we collect 676K samples from the generation-selection category (without tool calling) of Nemotron3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025), with responses generated by GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025). In total, the competition math SFT comprises 1.8M tool-calling samples and 2.6M samples without tool use.

对于数学自然语言证明，我们从 Nemotron-Math-Proofs-v1 的 AOPS 分裂中收集了 98K 数学证明问题（Du et al., 2025）。我们为每个问题生成多个样本，以涵盖使用 DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale（Liu 等人，2025）的证明生成（410K）和证明验证（400K）两种功能，总共产生 816K 个样本。For mathematical natural language proof, we collect 98K mathematical proof problems from the AOPS split of Nemotron-Math-Proofs-v1 (Du et al., 2025). We generate multiple samples per problem to cover two capabilities including proof generation (410K) and proof verification (400K) using DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale (Liu et al., 2025), resulting in a total of 816K samples.

3.2.2.代码推理3.2.2. Code Reasoning

我们基于 Nemotron-Cascade 1（Wang 等人，2025）构建，从多个开源数据集收集了大约 165K 个独特的编码提示，包括 OpenCode-Stage2（Huang 等人，2024）、OpenCodeReasoning（Ahmad 等人，2025）和 HardTests（He 等人，2025）。这些提示最初源自 Codeforces、AtCoder、AIZU 和 CodeChef 等竞争性编程平台。为了鼓励 SFT 训练集中的多样性并减少冗余，我们使用两种方法应用严格的重复数据删除：(1) 样本 I/O 指纹识别和 (2) 基于 n-gram 的文本分析。此过程删除了大约 24.2% 的自我重复编码提示。Built on Nemotron-Cascade 1 (Wang et al., 2025), we curate approximately 165K unique coding prompts from several open-source datasets, including OpenCode-Stage2 (Huang et al., 2024), OpenCodeReasoning (Ahmad et al., 2025), and HardTests (He et al., 2025). These prompts are originally sourced from competitive programming platforms such as Codeforces, AtCoder, AIZU, and CodeChef. To encourage prompt diversity and reduce redundancy in our SFT training set, we apply strict deduplication using two methods: (1) sample I/O fingerprinting and (2) n-gram-based text analysis. This process removes approximately 24.2% of self-duplicated coding prompts.

7

Nemotron-Cascade 2：采用 Cascade RL 和多域按策略蒸馏的训练后法学硕士Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

由于其强大的代码推理能力，我们选择 GPT-OSS-120B（Agarwal et al., 2025）作为我们的 SFT 教师模型。对于具有可验证测试用例的每个编码提示，我们对教师的推理轨迹应用正确性过滤，仅保留那些生成正确代码的轨迹。对于没有可验证测试用例的提示，我们通常选择较长的推理轨迹，假设它们反映了更彻底的问题分析。该管道生成最终数据集，其中包含 1.9M Python 推理跟踪、1.0M C++14 推理跟踪和 1.3M Python 工具调用推理跟踪，用于竞争性编码。We choose GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025) as our SFT teacher model due to its strong code reasoning capabilities. For each coding prompt with verifiable test cases, we apply correctness filtering to the teacher’s reasoning traces, retaining only those that generate correct code. For prompts without verifiable test cases, we generally select longer reasoning traces under the assumption that they reflect more thorough problem analysis. This pipeline yields a final dataset comprising 1.9M Python reasoning traces, 1.0M C++14 reasoning traces, and 1.3M Python tool-calling reasoning traces for competitive coding.

科学编码：Scientific Coding:我们进一步收集涵盖生物学、材料科学、物理、化学和数学领域的科学研究编码提示。对这些提示的响应由 GPT-OSS-120B（Agarwal 等人，2025）生成，总共产生 110 万个 SFT 样本。We further collect scientific research coding prompts spanning the domains of biology, material science, physics, chemistry, and mathematics. The responses to these prompts are generated by GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025), resulting in a total of 1.1M SFT samples.

3.2.3.科学3.2.3. Science

科学提示我们跨物理、化学和生物学进行收集。我们使用来自 Nemotron-Cascade（Wang 等人，2025）的 1.4M 科学 SFT 样本和来自 Nemotron-3-Nano（Blakeman 等人，2025）的额外 1.3M 样本。两个数据集中的响应均由 GPT-OSS-120B 生成（Agarwal 等人，2025）。The science prompts we collect span physics, chemistry, and biology. We use 1.4M science SFT samples from Nemotron-Cascade (Wang et al., 2025) and an additional 1.3M samples from Nemotron-3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025). Responses in both datasets are generated by GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025).

3.2.4.长上下文3.2.4. Long Context

我们采用 Nemotron-3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025) 的 160K 长上下文 SFT 数据，其平均序列长度为 128K token。此外，我们从 ChatQA-2（Xu 等人，2024）收集了另一个 74K 长上下文 SFT，其平均长度为 29K 个标记。We adopt the 160K long context SFT data from Nemotron-3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025), which has an average sequence length of 128K tokens. In addition, we collect another 74K long context SFT from ChatQA-2 (Xu et al., 2024), which has an average length of 29K tokens.

3.2.5.一般聊天3.2.5. General Chat

我们从 Nemotron-Cascade 1（Wang 等人，2025）获取提示，并构建 490 万个推理开启样本和 372K 个推理关闭样本。推理样本的响应由 GPT-OSS-120B 生成（Agarwal 等人，2025）。对于推理样本，300K 响应是从数据集本身内的高质量带注释的简短答案中提取的，而另外 330K 则由 DeepSeek-V3-0324（Liu 等人，2024）生成，以提高响应质量。We source prompts from Nemotron-Cascade 1 (Wang et al., 2025) and construct 4.9M reasoning-on and 372K reasoning-off samples. Responses for reasoning-on samples are generated by GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025). For reasoning-off samples, 300K responses are drawn from high-quality annotated short answers within the dataset itself, while an additional 330K are generated by DeepSeek-V3-0324 (Liu et al., 2024) to improve response quality.

为了增强多轮对话功能，我们在角色扮演设置中使用两个 GPT-OSS-120B（Agarwal 等人，2025）实例合成了大约 700K 多轮对话样本，其中一个实例扮演用户，另一个实例扮演助手。用户端模型可以随时终止对话以防止重复交换。To enhance multi-turn dialogue capabilities, we synthesize approximately 700K multi-turn conversation samples using two GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025) instances in a role-playing setup, where one instance plays the user and the other the assistant. The user-side model may terminate the conversation at any point to prevent repetitive exchanges.

我们还整合了来自 Nemotron-3-Nano（Blakeman 等人，2025）的 460 万个推理聊天样本，以及来自 LMSYS（Zheng 等人，2023）和 WildChat（Zhao 等人，2024）的提示。响应由 GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025)、Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 和 Qwen3-235BA22B-Instruct-2507 (Yang et al., 2025) 生成。We additionally incorporate 4.6M reasoning-on chat samples from Nemotron-3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025), with prompts drawn from LMSYS (Zheng et al., 2023) and WildChat (Zhao et al., 2024). Responses are generated by GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025), Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507, and Qwen3-235BA22B-Instruct-2507 (Yang et al., 2025).

3.2.6.遵循指令3.2.6. Instruction Following

In addition, we incorporate 497K instruction-following samples from Nemotron-3Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025), including 457K reasoning-on and 40K reasoning-off responses. These responses are generated by GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025), Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507, and Qwen3-235BA22B-Instruct-2507 (Yang et al., 2025).

3.2.7.安全3.2.7. Safety

我们从 Nemotron-3-Nano（Blakeman 等人，2025）收集 4K 安全 SFT 样本，以使模型在遇到不安全输入时表现出适当的拒绝行为。 SFT 提示最初源自 Nemotron Content Safety v2（Ghosh 等人，2025）、Gretel Safety Alignment v1（gre，2024）、Harmful Tasks（Hasan 等人，2024）和 Red-Team-2K（Luo 等人，2024）。We collect 4K safety SFT samples from Nemotron-3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025) to enable models to exhibit appropriate refusal behavior when encountering unsafe inputs. The SFT prompts are originally sourced from Nemotron Content Safety v2 (Ghosh et al., 2025), Gretel Safety Alignment v1 (gre, 2024), Harmful Tasks (Hasan et al., 2024), and Red-Team-2K (Luo et al., 2024).

3.2.8.会话代理3.2.8. Conversational Agent

除了用于数学和代码推理的 Python 工具使用数据外，我们还进一步收集多轮对话设置中的工具使用样本，其中有多种工具可用，助手必须确定要调用哪些工具以及如何有效使用它们。我们从 Nemotron3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025) 收集了 822K 个会话工具使用样本，并由 Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507、Qwen3-32B、Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507 (Yang et al., 2025) 和 GPT-OSS-120B 生成响应（阿加瓦尔等人，2025）。Aside from the Python tool-use data for math and code reasoning, we further gather tool-use samples in multi-turn conversational settings, where multiple tools are available and the assistant must determine which tools to invoke and how to use them effectively. We collect 822K conversational tool-use samples from Nemotron3-Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025), with responses generated by Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507, Qwen3-32B, Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507 (Yang et al., 2025), and GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025).

3.2.9.软件工程代理3.2.9. Software Engineering Agent

我们使用各种代理支架来管理软件工程（SWE）数据，包括 OpenHands（Wang 等人，2025）、SWE-Agent（Yang 等人，2024）、Mini-SWE-Agent 以及 Wei 等人提出的无代理支架。 （2025），增强模型的代理软件工程能力。首先，我们利用 Nemotron 3 Nano（Blakeman 等人，2025）和 Super（Blakeman 等人，2025）的数据，其中包括使用 Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct（Yang 等人，2025）生成的 SWE 代理轨迹。问题实例来自 SWE-Gym (Pan* et al., 2025)、SWE-rebench (Badertdinov et al., 2025) 和 R2E-Subset (Jain et al., 2025)。其次，我们采用来自 Nemotron-Cascade 1（Wang 等人，2025）的 SWE 无代理数据，其中包括三个主要任务：（1）错误代码本地化，（2）代码修复和（3）测试用例生成。遵循 Wang 等人的既定程序。 (2025)，我们使用 DeepSeek-V3.2 重建代码修复数据 (Liu et al., 2025)。We curate the software engineering (SWE) data using various agentic scaffolds, including OpenHands (Wang et al., 2025), SWE-Agent (Yang et al., 2024), Mini-SWE-Agent, and the agentless scaffold proposed by Wei et al. (2025), to enhance the models’ agentic software engineering capabilities. First, we utilize the data from Nemotron 3 Nano (Blakeman et al., 2025) and Super (Blakeman et al., 2025), which includes SWE agentic trajectories generated using Qwen3-Coder-480B-A35B-Instruct (Yang et al., 2025). The problem instances are drawn from SWE-Gym (Pan* et al., 2025), SWE-rebench (Badertdinov et al., 2025), and R2E-Subset (Jain et al., 2025). Second, we employ SWE agentless data from Nemotron-Cascade 1 (Wang et al., 2025), which includes three main tasks: (1) buggy code localization, (2) code repair, and (3) test case generation. Following the established procedure in Wang et al. (2025), we reconstruct the code repair data using DeepSeek-V3.2 (Liu et al., 2025).

我们的初步研究表明，结合 SWE 无代理数据可以提高模型在 SWE 代理任务上的有效性。例如，仅对代理数据进行微调可实现 Pass@1 的 48.9 和 Pass@4 的 62.8，而对代理和无代理数据的组合进行微调可将性能提高到使用 OpenHands 验证的 SWE-bench 上的 Pass@1 的 49.9 和 Pass@4 的 65.2。基于这一观察，我们将 125K 个代理样本和 389K 个无代理样本组合起来作为 SWE 任务的监督微调（SFT）数据。我们的模型在 SWE 代理数据上以非思维模式进行训练，并在 SWE 无代理数据上以思维模式进行训练。Our preliminary study shows that incorporating SWE agentless data improves models’ effectiveness on SWE agentic tasks. For example, fine-tuning solely on agentic data achieves Pass@1 of 48.9 and Pass@4 of 62.8, whereas fine-tuning on a combination of agentic and agentless data improves performance to Pass@1 of 49.9 and Pass@4 of 65.2 on SWE-bench Verified using OpenHands. Based on this observation, we combine 125K agentic samples and 389K agentless samples as the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data for SWE tasks. Our models are trained in non-thinking mode on SWE agentic data and in thinking mode on SWE agentless data.

3.2.10.终端代理3.2.10. Terminal Agent

为了增强终端使用的代理能力，我们采用To enhance agentic capabilities for terminal use, we adopt the终端任务生成Terminal-Task-Gen方法（Pi 等人，2026）来策划我们的培训任务。该框架由 (1) 将静态数据转换为交互式终端格式的数据集适配器和 (2) 从不同的种子提示和结构化终端技能分类生成的综合任务组成。使用这个框架，我们总共管理了 49 万个样本。具体来说，我们首先改编来自现有高质量来源的 162K 数学、32K 代码和 32K SWE 特定样本（Wang 等人，2025），从而建立了广泛的基础覆盖范围。为了进一步提高有针对性的技能细化，我们综合了 12 万个基于种子的任务和 14 万个基于技​​能的任务。对于轨迹构建，我们利用上面策划的任务，并采用 DeepSeek-V3.2（Liu 等人，2025）作为核心引擎，通过隔离 Docker 环境中的执行反馈循环生成逐步解决方案跟踪。 Terminus 2 代理框架（Merrill 等人，2026）充当底层脚手架和工具使用协议，使模型能够与终端交互并完成复杂的任务。methodology (Pi et al., 2026) to curate our training tasks. This framework consists of (1) dataset adapters that transform static data into interactive terminal formats, and (2) synthetic tasks generated from both diverse seed prompts and a structured terminal skill taxonomy. Using this framework, we curate 490K samples in total. Specifically, we first adapt 162K math, 32K code, and 32K SWE-specific samples from existing high-quality sources (Wang et al., 2025), which establishes broad foundational coverage. To further improve targeted skill refinement, we synthesize 120K seed-based and 140K skill-based tasks. For trajectory construction, we leverage the tasks curated from above, and employ DeepSeek-V3.2 (Liu et al., 2025) as the core engine to generate step-by-step solution traces via an execution-feedback loop within isolated Docker environments. The Terminus 2 agent framework (Merrill et al., 2026) serves as the underlying scaffolding and tool-use protocol, enabling the model to interact with the terminal and complete complex tasks.

4.级联强化学习和多域同策略蒸馏4. Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

遵循与 Nemotron-Cascade 1（Wang 等人，2025）类似的方法，我们应用级联强化学习（Cascade RL）作为我们的后训练管道。Following a similar approach to Nemotron-Cascade 1 (Wang et al., 2025), we apply Cascaded Reinforcement Learning (Cascade RL) as our post training pipeline.

4.1.培训框架4.1. Training Framework

In this work, we start the Cascade RL process with IF-RL (§4.2) to establish foundational instruction adherence, followed by multi-domain RL (§4.3) to enhance the model’s tool-calling capabilities, STEM reasoning, and response format adherence. We then transition to Multi-domain On-policy Distillation (§4.4) to unify specialized expertise into a single, cohesive policy to mitigate performance degradation. 4.1.1. Cascade RL 的顺序由什么决定4.1.1. What determines the ordering of Cascade RL

Cascade RL 流水线中各阶段的最佳顺序并不是一个通用常数；相反，它是模型基本行为和学习轨迹的动态函数。与最初的 Nemotron Cascade（Wang 等人，2025）相比，我们当前的工作 Nemotron-Cascade 2 显着改进了 SFT 数据质量，并大幅降低了 RL 环境和任务的复杂性。这些进步从根本上改变了模型的行为动态，这要求我们采用不同的顺序，以更好地适应法学硕士不断发展的能力。The optimal ordering of stages within a Cascade RL pipeline is not a universal constant; rather, it is a dynamic function of the model’s underlying behaviors and learning trajectories. In contrast to the original Nemotron Cascade (Wang et al., 2025), our current work Nemotron-Cascade 2 introduces significant improvements in SFT data quality and substantially scales the complexity of the RL environments and tasks. These advancements have fundamentally altered the model’s behavioral dynamics, which require us to adopt a different order to better accommodate the evolving capabilities of LLMs.

经验法则：减轻域间干扰。Rule of thumb: Mitigating Inter-Domain Interference.具体来说，这种排序的基本原理主要是由于模型与日益多样化的环境交互时需要减轻灾难性遗忘。 Cascade RL 提供了一个精细的镜头，通过它我们可以观察特定领域如何竞争或冲突，例如 IF-RL 中的严格指令遵守与 RLHF 中的人类偏好对齐。我们的核心设计原则是确定一种排序，最大限度地减少跨域的负面干扰，同时彻底优化最高优先级的域。通过确定哪些任务作为基础先验，哪些任务作为专门的改进，我们可以减轻域间干扰。Specifically, the rationale for this ordering is primarily driven by the need to mitigate catastrophic forgetting as the model interacts with increasingly diverse environments. Cascade RL provides a granular lens through which we can observe how specific domains compete or conflict, such as strict instruction adherence in IF-RL versus human preference alignment in RLHF. Our core design principle is to identify an ordering that minimizes negative interference across domains while thoroughly optimizing the highest-priority domains. By identifying which tasks serve as foundational priors and which act as specialized refinements, we can mitigate inter-domain interference.

通过多域集成进行扩展。Scaling via Multi-Domain Integration.遵循这一原则，当发现特定域不冲突或有利于整体性能时，Cascade RL 管道可以合并多域 RL 阶段。随着 RL 环境和数据集变得越来越复杂，这种集成方法特别有效，同时确保模型在各种基准上保持广泛的性能概况，如第 4.3 节中详述。Following this principle, the Cascade RL pipeline can incorporate multi-domain RL stages when specific domains are found to be non-conflicting or beneficial to the overall performance. This integrated approach is particularly effective as RL environments and datasets grow in complexity, while ensuring that the model maintains a broad performance profile across various benchmarks, as detailed in §4.3.

通过在政策蒸馏实现稳定。Stabilization through On-policy Distillation.此外，我们发现多域同策略蒸馏（第 4.4 节）是该排序中的关键稳定点。它可以有效地恢复在级联强化学习的早期、更专业阶段可能出现退化的基准性能，从而形成更加平衡和稳健的最终政策模型。Furthermore, We find that Multi-domain On-policy Distillation (§4.4) serves as a critical stabilization point in this ordering. It is effective at recovering benchmark performance that may have regressed during earlier, more specialized stages of the cascade RL, leading to a more balanced and robust final policy model.

4.1.2.强化学习训练配置4.1.2. RL Training Configuration

在整个级联强化学习过程中，我们使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）算法（Shao et al., 2024），并严格遵循Throughout the entire Cascade RL process, we use Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm (Shao et al., 2024) with strict在政策on-policy按照 Nemotron Cascade 进行训练（Wang 等人，2025）。我们采用on-policy训练来提高稳定性和准确性。我们使用 Nemo-RL 存储库（NVIDIA，2025）进行训练。training following Nemotron Cascade (Wang et al., 2025). We adopt on-policy training for improved stability and higher accuracy. We conduct our training using the Nemo-RL repository (NVIDIA, 2025).

Nemotron-Cascade 2：采用 Cascade RL 和多域按策略蒸馏的训练后法学硕士Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

更新。这确保了用于数据收集的策略始终与正在更新的策略相匹配，从而使重要性采样比恰好为 1。这种策略内设置有助于稳定的 RL 训练并减轻熵崩溃。此外，我们完全删除了 KL 散度项，这将 GRPO 目标简化为标准update. This ensures that the policy used for data collection always matches the one being updated, making the importance sampling ratio exactly 1. This on-policy setup contributes to stable RL training and mitigates entropy collapse. In addition, we remove KL divergence term entirely, which simplifies the GRPO objective to the standard强化REINFORCE具有群体标准化奖励和代币级别损失的目标（Williams，1992）（Yu et al.，2025）：objective (Williams, 1992) with group-normalized rewards and token-level loss (Yu et al., 2025):

4.2.指令跟随强化学习 (IF-RL)4.2. Instruction-Following Reinforcement Learning (IF-RL)

在本小节中，我们将描述遵循指令的 RL 配方，它作为 Cascade RL 的第一阶段。我们证明，应用可验证的 IF-RL 可以显着提高指令依从性，在 IFBench 上实现 83.13% 的最先进准确率（Pyatkin 等人，2025）。In this subsection, we describe our instruction-following RL recipe, which serves as the first stage of our Cascade RL. We demonstrate that applying verifiable IF-RL significantly improves instruction adherence, achieving a state-of-the-art accuracy of 83.13% on IFBench (Pyatkin et al., 2025).

4.2.1.数据集4.2.1. Dataset

我们使用与 NVIDIA Nano-v3 后期训练相同的指令跟踪训练数据（Blakeman 等人，2025）。该数据集中的说明​​是为了客观可验证性而设计的，例如，要求响应少于 200 个字。这使得该数据集非常适合严格遵守训练和评估模型。鉴于数据的高基线质量，我们的管理过程主要解决某些指令类型（例如 count_increment_word）的关键字参数内的格式不一致问题。We use the same instruction-following training data used for NVIDIA Nano-v3 post-training (Blakeman et al., 2025). The instructions in this dataset are designed for objective verifiability, for instance, requiring a response to be under 200 words. This making the dataset well-suited for training and evaluating models on strict adherence. Given the high baseline quality of the data, our curation process mainly resolves formatting inconsistencies within the keyword arguments for certain instruction types (e.g., count_increment_word).

4.2.2.训练食谱4.2.2. Training recipe

继（Wang et al., 2025）之后，我们还应用了动态过滤（Yu et al., 2025）。该技术过滤掉所有推出完全正确或完全错误的样本。通过确保批次中的每个提示都提供有效的梯度，动态过滤可以稳定 IF-RL 训练并推动模型性能的上限。此外，我们观察到扩展的 IF-RL 训练可能会导致过度使用令牌，而这对于满足一般聊天领域的特定约束通常是不必要的。为了缓解这种情况，我们应用了超长惩罚，即对未能在最大序列长度内完成生成的样本进行零奖励的惩罚。Following (Wang et al., 2025), we also apply dynamic filtering (Yu et al., 2025). This technique filters out samples where all rollouts are either entirely correct or entirely incorrect. By ensuring that every prompt in a batch provides effective gradients, dynamic filtering stabilizes IF-RL training and pushes the upper bound of model performance. Furthermore, we observed that extended IF-RL training can lead to excessive token usage, which is often unnecessary for fulfilling specific constraints in general chat domains. To mitigate this, we apply overlong penalty, which penalizes samples that fail to complete generation within the maximum sequence length with a zero reward.

与 Nemotron Cascade（Wang 等人，2025）不同，我们将 IF-RL 定位为 Cascade RL 训练的第一阶段，主要有两个原因：（i）IF-RL 会对人类对齐能力（例如 ArenaHard）产生负面影响，而我们后续基于生成奖励模型的 RLHF 对指令跟踪分数的影响可以忽略不计。通过首先优先考虑指令遵守，我们可以专注于最大化指令遵循性能，然后利用后期阶段来恢复和完善人类偏好一致性。 (ii) 早期的 IF-RL 阶段产生了具有卓越指令跟踪能力的模型，为后续的多域同策略蒸馏提供了强有力的指导。与 Nemotron Cascade（Wang et al., 2025）的另一个区别是，我们的 IF-RL 仅在“思维模式”下进行训练，而没有纳入奖励模型。我们发现“思维模式”在指令跟踪基准测试（例如 IFBench（Pyatkin 等人，2025））上产生更高的准确性。由于后续的 RL 阶段会恢复 IF-RL 期间引入的人类偏好对齐的任何回归，因此我们可以完全专注于最大化指令依从性，而不会产生辅助奖励模型的计算开销。Unlike Nemotron Cascade (Wang et al., 2025), we position IF-RL as the first stage of our Cascade RL training for two primary reasons: (i) IF-RL can negatively impact human alignment capabilities (e.g., ArenaHard), while our subsequent generative-reward-model-based RLHF has a negligible impact on instruction following scores. By prioritizing instruction adherence first, we can focus on maximizing instruction following performance and then utilize the later stages to recover and refine human preference alignment. (ii) An early IF-RL stage produces a model with superior instruction-following capabilities, which serves as a strong teacher for subsequent multi-domain on-policy distillation. Another difference from Nemotron Cascade (Wang et al., 2025) is that our IF-RL is trained exclusively in “thinking mode” without incorporating a reward model. We found that the “thinking mode” yields higher accuracy on instruction-following benchmarks (e.g., IFBench (Pyatkin et al., 2025)). Because subsequent RL stages recover any regressions in human preference alignment introduced during IF-RL, we can focus entirely on maximizing instruction adherence without incurring the computational overhead of an auxiliary reward model.

我们使用的批量大小为 128，每个提示采样 16 个响应，温度为 1.0，top-p 1.0。我们采用 AdamW (Kingma, 2014) 的学习率为 2e-6，并设置熵损失系数和 KL 损失We use a batch size of 128, sampling 16 responses per prompt with temperature 1.0 and top-p 1.0. We adopt a learning rate of 2e-6 with AdamW (Kingma, 2014), and set both the entropy loss coefficient and KL loss

系数为 0。我们的具有动态滤波功能的 IF-RL 大约需要 180 个步骤。附录 B 中提供了全套超参数。coefficient to 0. Our IF-RL with dynamic filtering takes around 180 steps. The full set of hyperparameters is provided in Appendix B.

4.3.多域强化学习4.3. Multi-domain RL

继 IF-RL 之后，我们进行了多域 RL 的额外阶段，涵盖三种功能：STEM 域中的多选题回答 (MCQA)、代理工具调用和指令跟踪的结构化输出。数据集取自 NVIDIA Nano-v3 RL 训练混合（Blakeman 等人，2025）。数据混合物由大约 55% 的 MCQA、30% 使用 Workplace Assistant 设置的代理工具调用（Blakeman 等人，2025）和 15% 的结构化输出组成。Following IF-RL, we conduct an additional stage of multi-domain RL that covers three capabilities: multi-choice question answering (MCQA) in the STEM domain, agentic tool calling, and structured output for instruction following. The datasets are drawn from the NVIDIA Nano-v3 RL training blend (Blakeman et al., 2025). The data mixture consists of approximately 55% MCQA, 30% agentic tool calling using the Workplace Assistant setup (Blakeman et al., 2025), and 15% structured output.

我们将这些域分组为一个多域强化学习阶段，主要有两个原因。首先，在混合域上进行训练时，我们没有观察到评估基准的性能下降。相反，该模型在包括 MMLU-Pro、We group these domains into a single multi-domain RL stage for two main reasons. First, we do not observe performance degradation across evaluation benchmarks when training on the blended domains. Instead, the model exhibits consistent improvements on benchmarks including MMLU-Pro,𝜏[2] -Bench 和 IF-Bench。其次，这些数据集的响应长度和验证时间相似，这最大限度地减少了因等待较长代或较慢的环境验证而导致的训练效率低下。[2] -Bench, and IF-Bench. Second, the response lengths and verification times of these datasets are similar, which minimizes training inefficiencies caused by waiting for longer generations or slower environment verification.

在训练期间，我们使用 128 的批量大小，并在温度 1.0 和 top-p 1.0 的情况下对每个提示采样 16 个响应（参见附录 B）。我们采用学习率3During training, we use a batch size of 128 and sample 16 responses per prompt with temperature 1.0 and top-p 1.0 (see Appendix B). We adopt a learning rate of 3× 10 [−][6] 与 AdamW (Kingma, 2014) 一起使用，并将熵损失系数和 KL 损失系数设置为零。这个多域 RL 阶段运行大约 70 个训练步骤。[6] with AdamW (Kingma, 2014), and set both the entropy loss coefficient and KL loss coefficient to zero. This multi-domain RL stage runs for approximately 70 training steps.

4.4.多域同策略蒸馏 (MOPD)4.4. Multi-domain On-Policy Distillation (MOPD)

虽然与任意顺序的普通顺序强化学习相比，精心设计的级联强化学习可以大大减少灾难性遗忘，但随着训练环境数量的增加，它并不能完全消除能力漂移。在实践中，我们观察到在整个训练过程中跟踪的不同基准类别之间存在明显的波动，并且主要的权衡因阶段而异。例如，某些 RLVR 训练通常会降低模型熵并缩短推理轨迹，从而对数学推理性能产生负面影响，而面向 RLHF 的优化可以部分抵消指令跟踪行为。这些观察结果激发了额外的训练阶段，以在级联强化学习过程中重新平衡能力。While well-designed Cascade RL substantially reduces catastrophic forgetting compared with vanilla sequential RL in an arbitrary order, it does not fully eliminate capability drift as the number of training environments increases. In practice, we observe noticeable fluctuations across different benchmark categories tracked throughout training, and the dominant trade-offs differ by stage. For example, certain RLVR training often reduces model entropy and shortens reasoning traces, thus can negatively impact mathematical reasoning performance, while RLHF-oriented optimization can partially trade off against instruction-following behavior. These observations motivate an additional training stage for re-balancing capabilities within the Cascade RL process.

因此我们采用We therefore adopt多域同策略蒸馏（MOPD）multi-domain on-policy distillation (MOPD)（Agarwal et al., 2024；Lu and Lab, 2025；Xiao et al., 2026；Yang et al., 2025；Zeng et al., 2026）作为补充的训练后阶段。在我们的环境中，MOPD 由于三个原因而特别有吸引力。首先，通过为每个基准类别选择最强的验证检查点，可以直接从 Cascade RL 管道中选择教师检查点，这使得可以轻松组装能力多样化的教师池，而无需引入外部模型系列。其次，由于这些教师源自相同的 SFT 初始化，因此他们与学生共享相同的分词器和词汇，从而减少了分布偏移并避免了额外的对齐问题。第三，MOPD 提供了密集的代币级别的训练优势，这与稀疏的结果奖励相比特别有用，在图 3(c) 中，我们展示了它与 GRPO 相比的训练效率优势。(Agarwal et al., 2024; Lu and Lab, 2025; Xiao et al., 2026; Yang et al., 2025; Zeng et al., 2026) as a complementary post-training stage. In our setting, MOPD is particularly attractive for three reasons. First, teacher checkpoints can be selected directly from the Cascade RL pipeline by choosing the strongest validation checkpoint for each benchmark category, which makes it easy to assemble a capability-diverse teacher pool without introducing external model families. Second, because these teachers are derived from the same SFT initialization, they share the same tokenizer and vocabulary as the student, reducing distribution shift and avoiding additional alignment issues. Third, MOPD provides a dense token-level training advantage, which is especially useful compared with sparse outcome rewards, and in Figure 3(c) we show its training-efficiency benefits compared with GRPO.

培训效率优势。Training efficiency advantage.

MOPD 提供了密集的代币级蒸馏优势，而 GRPO 依赖于所有生成的代币共享的稀疏序列级结果奖励。这使得 MOPD 在实践中的样本和步骤效率大大提高。从相同的初始检查点开始，MOPD 始终以更少的优化步骤达到更强的性能。在 AIME25 上（图 3(c)），在纯数学训练下，GRPO 在 25 步后从 89.9 提高到 91.0，而 MOPD 在 30 步内达到 92.0，恢复了教师水平的表现。 ArenaHard v2.0 上也出现了类似的趋势（表 3）。 52 个步骤后，MOPD 将硬提示从 71.5 提高MOPD provides a dense token-level distillation advantage, whereas GRPO relies on a sparse sequence-level outcome reward that is shared across all generated tokens. This makes MOPD substantially more sample- and step-efficient in practice. Starting from the same initial checkpoint, MOPD consistently reaches stronger performance in fewer optimization steps. On AIME25 (Figure 3(c)), under math-only training, GRPO improves from 89.9 to 91.0 after 25 steps, while MOPD reaches 92.0 within 30 steps and recovers teacher-level performance. 4.5.根据人类反馈进行强化学习 (RLHF)4.5. Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF)

我们的 RLHF 配方建立在多域同策略蒸馏的基础上，重点关注人类偏好学习。根据 ArenaHard v2 的衡量，这一过程进一步增强了编码和数学方面的创造性写作和不可验证的问题解决能力，同时保持了其他领域的性能而不会降低。Building on multi-domain on-policy distillation, our RLHF recipe focuses on human preferece learning. This process further enhances creative writing and non-verifiable problem-solving in coding and mathematics, as measured by ArenaHard v2, while maintaining performance across other domains without degradation.

4.5.1.数据集4.5.1. Dataset

我们采用 NVIDIA Nano-v3 的 RLHF 训练数据集（Blakeman 等人，2025），其中包括 HelpSteer3（Wang 等人，2025）、arena- human-preference-140k 数据集的商业友好子集（Chiang 等人，2024）以及合成安全混合物（Blakeman 等人，2025）。继 NVIDIA Nano-v3（Blakeman 等人，2025）之后，我们利用 Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 作为我们的生成奖励模型（GenRM），通过 HelpSteer3 框架进行训练（Wang 等人，2025）。给定对话历史记录、用户请求和两个候选响应，GenRM 首先对每个响应的优点和缺点进行推理，然后生成个人有用性分数和最终的比较排名。We adopt the RLHF training dataset from NVIDIA Nano-v3 (Blakeman et al., 2025), which comprises HelpSteer3 (Wang et al., 2025), a commercially-friendly subset of the arena-human-preference-140k dataset (Chiang et al., 2024), and a synthetic safety blend (Blakeman et al., 2025). Following the NVIDIA Nano-v3 (Blakeman et al., 2025), we utilize Qwen3-235B-A22B-Thinking-2507 as our generative reward model (GenRM), trained via the HelpSteer3 framework (Wang et al., 2025). Given a conversation history, a user request, and two candidate responses, the GenRM first reasons through the strengths and weaknesses of each response before producing individual helpfulness scores and a final comparative ranking.

4.5.2.训练食谱4.5.2. Training recipe

遵循类似于 NVIDIA Nano-v3（Blakeman 等人，2025）的训练方法，我们使用 GenRM 进行 RLHF。为了确保训练信号的高质量，我们对每个提示的所有转出对采用成对比较。我们以与 NVIDIA Nano-v3 RLHF 训练相同的方式聚合奖励分数，并应用相同的长度归一化奖励调整和质量门控简洁性奖励（Blakeman 等人，2025）。这些机制鼓励在不牺牲质量的情况下缩短响应时间，有效缓解推理令牌使用的快速增长。Following a training recipe similar to NVIDIA Nano-v3 (Blakeman et al., 2025), we conduct RLHF using the GenRM. To ensure the training signals are of high quality, we adopt pair-wise comparisons for all pairs of rollouts per prompt. We aggreagte the reward scores in the same way as NVIDIA Nano-v3 RLHF training, and apply the same length-normalized reward adjustment and quality-gated conciseness bonus (Blakeman et al., 2025). These mechanisms encourage shorter responses without sacrificing quality, effectively mitigating the rapid growth of inference token usage.

与 Nemotron Cascade (Wang et al., 2025) 不同，我们专门以思维模式训练 RLHF。虽然结合思维和非思维模式可以提高训练收敛性并在评估基准上产生轻微的收益，但我们观察到指令遵循性能的显着下降。由此产生的下降幅度足以使早期 RLVR 阶段获得的收益无法完全恢复。Different from Nemotron Cascade (Wang et al., 2025), we train RLHF exclusively in the thinking mode. While incorporating both thinking and non-thinking modes can improve training convergence and yield slight gains on evaluation benchmarks, we observe a significant degradation in instruction-following performance. The resulting drop is substantial enough that the gains obtained in the earlier RLVR stage cannot be fully recovered.

4.5.3.超参数4.5.3. Hyper-parameters

我们使用的批量大小为 128，每个提示生成 16 个 rollout，温度为 1.0，top-p 值为 1.0。我们在 RLHF 期间使用最大响应长度 16K，而不应用超长滤波。我们采用 AdamW 的学习率为 3e-6（Kingma，2014）。我们将熵损失系数设置为 0，KL 损失系数设置为 0.03，以保持模型在其他领域的能力。训练大约需要 30 个步骤。We use a batch size of 128, generating 16 rollout per prompt with a temperature of 1.0 and a top-p value of 1.0. We use a maximum response length of 16K during RLHF without applying overlong filtering. We adopt a learning rate of 3e-6 with AdamW (Kingma, 2014). We set the entropy loss coefficient to 0 and the KL loss coefficient to 0.03 to keep the model capabilities on other domains. The training takes around 30 steps.

4.6.长上下文强化学习4.6. Long-context RL

在 RLHF 之后，我们进行了长上下文 RL 阶段，以进一步增强模型的长上下文理解和推理能力。我们使用 NVIDIA Nano-v3 RL 数据混合（Blakeman 等人，2025），但限制Following RLHF, we conduct a stage of long-context RL to further enhance the model’s long-context understanding and reasoning capabilities. We use the NVIDIA Nano-v3 RL data blend (Blakeman et al., 2025), but restrict

14

Nemotron-Cascade 2：采用 Cascade RL 和多域按策略蒸馏的训练后法学硕士Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

此阶段仅适用于长上下文数据集。在我们的实验中，在长上下文强化学习期间合并其他领域会对不相关基准的性能产生负面影响，从而激发了这种特定于领域的训练设置。this phase to long-context datasets only. In our experiments, incorporating other domains during long-context RL negatively affects performance on unrelated benchmarks, motivating this domain-specific training setup.

我们采用 Nemo-Gym RL 环境（NVIDIA，2025）并使用 Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507 作为 LLM 法官来评估问答任务的模型推出。在训练期间，输入序列限制为 32K 个 token，最大序列长度设置为 49K 个 token，而不应用超长过滤。We adopt the Nemo-Gym RL environment (NVIDIA, 2025) and use Qwen3-235B-A22B-Instruct-2507 as an LLM judge to evaluate model rollouts for question answering tasks. During training, input sequences are limited to 32K tokens, and the maximum sequence length is set to 49K tokens without applying overlength filtering.

我们使用 128 的批量大小进行训练，每个提示生成 16 个 rollouts，温度为 1.0，top-p 1.0。使用 AdamW (Kingma, 2014) 进行优化，学习率为 3We train with a batch size of 128, generating 16 rollouts per prompt with temperature 1.0 and top-p 1.0. Optimization is performed using AdamW (Kingma, 2014) with a learning rate of 3× 10 [−][6]，而熵和KL损失系数均设置为零。训练运行大约 30 个步骤，因为我们观察到超过此点生成的推理标记迅速增加。[6] , while both the entropy and KL loss coefficients are set to zero. Training runs for approximately 30 steps, as we observe a rapid increase in generated inference tokens beyond this point.

4.7.代码 RL4.7. Code RL

4.7.1.数据管理4.7.1. Data Curation

我们从 Nemotron-Cascade 编码语料库（Wang 等人，2025）构建了我们的 Code RL 训练集，其中包含来自现代竞争性编程平台（例如 AtCoder、Codeforces 和 AIZU）的编码提示，以及用于奖励验证的强大测试用例。为了提高训练效率并加强深度推理，我们积极过滤掉 GPT-OSS-120B 在 8 次部署中的所有 8 次中正确解决的提示，从而生成仅包含 3.5K 样本的紧凑最终集。我们发现高难度的提示与强大的测试用例相结合对于进一步提高模型性能至关重要。We construct our Code RL training set from the Nemotron-Cascade coding corpus (Wang et al., 2025), which contains coding prompts sourced from modern competitive programming platforms such as AtCoder, Codeforces, and AIZU with robust test cases for reward verification. To improve training efficiency and strengthen deep reasoning, we aggressively filter out prompts that GPT-OSS-120B solves correctly in all 8 of 8 rollouts, yielding a compact final set of only 3.5K samples. We find that high-difficulty prompts paired with strong test cases are critical for further boosting model performance.

4.7.2.培训详情4.7.2. Training Details

我们使用 128 的批量大小和 3 的学习率进行代码 RLWe conduct Code RL using a batch size of 128 and a learning rate of 3× 10 [−][6] 使用 AdamW 优化器。与 Nemotron-Cascade 相比，我们将 RL 期间的最大响应长度增加到 118K 个 token，并将每个样本的 rollout 数量增加到 16，使策略能够更好地捕获需要长推理轨迹的极其困难的问题上的稀疏奖励信号。我们采用严格的二元奖励函数来避免潜在的奖励黑客行为，并保持整个训练完全符合策略以确保稳定性。支持 128 的验证吞吐量[6] with the AdamW optimizer. Compared to Nemotron-Cascade, we increase the maximum response length during RL to 118K tokens and the number of rollouts per sample to 16, enabling the policy to better capture sparse reward signals on extremely difficult problems that require long reasoning traces. We adopt the strict binary reward function to avoid potential reward hacking and keep the whole training to be fully on-policy for stability. To support the resulting verification throughput of 128× 16 = 2 ,每个 RL 步骤执行 048 次代码，我们部署了一个异步奖励验证服务器，该服务器在 384 个 CPU 核心上在 427.2 秒内完成每批。048 code executions per RL step, we deploy an asynchronous reward verification server that completes each batch in 427.2 seconds across 384 CPU cores.

4.8.软件工程强化学习（SWE RL）4.8. Software Engineering Reinforcement Learning (SWE RL)

4.8.1.无代理强化学习4.8.1. Agentless RL

训练细节和超参数。Training Details and Hyperparameters.

为了增强模型的代码修复能力，我们采用与 Wang 等人相同的数据源。 (2025) 用于无代理代码修复强化学习 (RL) 训练。由于大多数实例不提供可执行的 Docker 环境，因此我们采用 GPTOOS-120B 作为奖励模型来评估模型生成的代码修复的质量。继王等人之后。 (2025)，对于每个实例，我们使用黄金本地化和检索到的前 5 个本地化构建提示，并过滤掉相对简单的样本。我们执行批量大小为 128 的无代理 SWE RLTo enhance the models’ code repair capability, we adopt the same data source as Wang et al. (2025) for agentless code repair reinforcement learning (RL) training. Since most instances do not provide executable Docker environments, we employ GPTOOS-120B as a reward model to evaluate the quality of code repairs generated by our models. Following Wang et al. (2025), for each instance we construct prompts using both the golden localization and the top-5 retrieved localizations, and filter out relatively easy samples. We perform agentless SWE RL with a batch size of 128× 16 = 2 ,048（128 个提示，每个提示 16 个卷展），最大序列长度为 98,304，学习率为 3048 (128 prompts with 16 rollouts per prompt), a maximum sequence length of 98,304, and a learning rate of 3× 10 [−][6] 使用 AdamW 优化器。我们对温度 1.0 和 top-p 1.0 的响应进行采样。在训练过程中，我们会掩盖提示的损失，因为没有任何一次推出会获得大于 0.5 的奖励。我们观察到这些困难的提示会降低无代理 SWE RL 训练的稳定性和有效性。我们的无代理强化学习训练通常会在 40-50 个步骤内收敛。[6] using the AdamW optimizer. We sample responses with temperature 1.0 and top-p 1.0. During training, we mask the loss for prompts for which none of the rollouts receives a reward greater than 0.5. We observe that these difficult prompts degrade the stability and effectiveness of agentless SWE RL training. Our agentless RL training typically converges within 40–50 steps.

4.8.2. Agentic SWE Scaffold 的基于执行的强化学习4.8.2. Execution-based RL for Agentic SWE Scaffold

现代软件工程代理依赖于协调存储库交互、工具调用、代码编辑和测试执行的脚手架框架。训练智能体在这些环境中有效运行不仅需要优化单个模型输出，还需要优化整个问题解决轨迹。为了解决这个问题，我们直接在代理 SWE 支架中应用可验证奖励强化学习 (RLVR)，从而实现整个代理工作流程的端到端优化。我们的培训环境集成了已建立的 OpenHands 框架（Wang 等人，2025），该框架提供结构化工具使用、存储库交互和迭代补丁生成。Modern software engineering agents rely on scaffolding frameworks that coordinate repository interaction, tool calling, code editing, and test execution. Training agents to operate effectively within these environments requires optimizing not only individual model outputs but the entire problem-solving trajectory. To address this, we apply Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) directly within agentic SWE scaffolds, enabling end-to-end optimization of the full agent workflow. Our training environments integrate established OpenHands frameworks (Wang et al., 2025), which provide structured tool usage, repository interaction, and iterative patch generation.

我们在完全可执行的软件环境中使用基于执行的强化学习来训练代理，其中每个阶段都对应于从 SWE-bench 等基准中解决软件问题实例。该代理在一个仪表存储库中运行，该存储库公开用于文件检查、搜索、代码编辑和测试执行的工具。代理生成的候选补丁在环境中执行，该环境从编译结果和单元测试结果中返回可验证的信号，从而无需人工注释即可实现自动奖励计算。通过 OpenHands 脚手架框架，代理迭代地定位缺陷、提出补丁并通过测试执行对其进行验证。环境反馈（包括编译错误、失败的测试或成功的测试通过）提供直接反映功能正确性的确定性奖励。We train agents using execution-based reinforcement learning in fully executable software environments, where each episode corresponds to resolving a software issue instance from benchmarks such as SWE-bench. The agent operates inside an instrumented repository that exposes tools for file inspection, search, code editing, and test execution. Candidate patches generated by the agent are executed within the environment, which returns verifiable signals from compilation results and unit test outcomes, enabling automatic reward computation without human annotation. Through the OpenHands scaffolding framework, the agent iteratively localizes defects, proposes patches, and validates them through test execution. Environment feedback—including compilation errors, failing tests, or successful test passes—provides deterministic rewards that directly reflect functional correctness.

具体来说，我们进行基于执行的代理强化学习，批量大小为 1024，对应于 16 个提示，每个提示有 64 个推出。最大上下文长度设置为 256k 个令牌，并且允许代理最多 200 次交互轮次，在代理编码问题解决期间提供更大的推理令牌预算。训练数据来自 SWE-Gym (Pan* et al., 2025) 和 R2E-Subset (Jain et al., 2025)。我们使用中间模型为每个实例生成 16 个部署，并使用验证管道对其进行评估。所有部署均通过验证（100% 准确度）的实例（表明问题过于简单）将从数据集中删除。对于没有任何一次部署通过验证（0% 准确率）的情况，表明问题非常困难，我们随机丢弃 90% 的此类情况，以减少它们在训练数据中的比例。Specifically, we conduct execution-based agentic reinforcement learning with a batch size of 1024, corresponding to 16 prompts with 64 rollouts per prompt. The maximum context length is set to 256k tokens, and the agent is allowed up to 200 interaction turns, providing a larger reasoning token budget during agentic coding problem solving. Training data is drawn from SWE-Gym (Pan* et al., 2025) and R2E-Subset (Jain et al., 2025). We generate 16 rollouts per instance using our intermediate model and evaluate them using the verification pipeline. Instances for which all rollouts pass verification (100% accuracy), indicating overly simple problems, are removed from the dataset. For instances where none of the rollouts pass verification (0% accuracy), indicating extremely difficult problems, we randomly discard 90% of such cases to reduce their proportion in the training data.

5.1.国际海事组织 20255.1. IMO 2025

6.1. IOI 2025 和 ICPC 世界总决赛 20256.1. IOI 2025 and ICPC World Finals 2025

对于 IOI 2025，我们采用了 Nemotron-Cascade 的 IOI 测试时间缩放管道（Wang 等人，2025），它可以被视为多轮For IOI 2025, we adapt the IOI Test-Time Scaling pipeline from Nemotron-Cascade (Wang et al., 2025), which can be viewed as a multi-round生成-选择-提交generate-select-submit该框架在 IOI 官方规则下利用模型的推理能力。每个子任务最多分配 50 轮。在每一轮中，我们促使模型生成 40 个候选解决方案，这些解决方案与 (1) 提交历史记录和前几轮的官方法官判决进行汇总，以及 (2) 从同一主要任务中的高分或完全解决的子任务中分享的见解。附录 C.1 中提供了完整的聊天模板。使用这种方法，我们在问题 3 和问题 4 上取得了满分，在最多 40 分内获得了 439.28 的金牌分数framework that exploits the model’s reasoning ability under IOI’s official rules. Each subtask is allotted at most 50 rounds. Within each round, we prompt our model to generate 40 candidate solutions, aggregated with (1) submission history with official judge verdicts from previous rounds, and (2) shared insights from high scored or fully solved subtasks within the same main task. The complete chat template is provided in Appendix C.1. Using this approach, we achieved full score on Problem 3 and 4, achieving a gold-medal score of 439.28 within at most 40×50 = 2000 个模型代，而 507.66 的分数是在 5000 个模型内可以达到的。值得注意的是，在需要设计和优化启发式算法的问题 2 上，我们的流程在短短 5 轮（最多 200 个模型生成）内就达到了 86 点以上，展示了自我细化和跨子任务洞察的有效性。50 = 2000 model generations, while the score of 507.66 is achievable within 5000 generations. Notably, on Problem 2 which requires designing and optimizing a heuristic algorithm, our pipeline reached over 86 points in just 5 rounds (at most 200 model generations), demonstrating the effectiveness of self-refinement and cross-subtask insights.

对于 2025 年 ICPC 世界总决赛，我们为每个问题生成最多 1000 个解决方案，并在初步筛选后提交给官方评估。我们成功解决了 12 个问题中的 10 个问题，获得了金牌第四名，仅在 100 份提交中就解决了 8 个问题（问题 A 和 I 除外）。For ICPC World Finals 2025, we generate up to 1000 solutions per problem and submit them for official evaluation after initial filtering. We successfully solved 10 out of 12 problems, achieving the #4 Gold medal placement, with 8 problems (except Problems A and I) solved within only 100 submissions.

我们在各种竞争性编码基准上评估我们的 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B 模型，包括 LiveCodeBench v6（Jain 等人，2024）和 LiveCodeBench Pro（Zheng 等人，2025）的 25Q1 和 25Q2 划分。我们还通过模拟参与来估算 Codeforces ELO 分数We evaluate our Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B model on various competitive coding benchmarks, including LiveCodeBench v6 (Jain et al., 2024), and LiveCodeBench Pro (Zheng et al., 2025)’s 25Q1 and 25Q2 splits. We also estimate Codeforces ELO score through simulated participation on40Div.1/Div.2 Codeforces 回合于 2501 年至 2507 年举行。我们报告了在 128K 代币思维预算、采样温度 1.0 和 top_p 0.95 下的 avg@8 结果。对于工具集成推理 (TIR) 结果，我们允许模型调用有状态的 Python 执行器，最多进行 100 次调用。对于基线模型评估，我们遵循他们推荐的推理配置，确保至少 128K 令牌到最多 256K 令牌的思维预算。更多评估详情请参见附录 A 和附录 D。Div.1/Div.2 Codeforces Rounds held from 2501 to 2507. We report our avg@8 results under 128K-token thinking budget, the sampling temperature of 1.0 and the top_p of 0.95. For Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) results, we allow our model to call a stateful Python executor for up to 100 calls. For baseline model evaluation, we follow their recommended inference configurations, ensuring a thinking budget of at least 128K tokens to at most 256K tokens. More evaluation details can be found in Appendix A and Appendix D.

120B-A12B、GPT-OSS-120B 和 Qwen-3.5-122B-A10B。通过工具集成推理（TIR），我们的模型的性能可以进一步提升，特别是在困难问题上，并匹配总参数超过300B的最强开源模型，例如Kimi-K2.5-1T-Thinking、Qwen-3.5-397B-A17B和DeepSeekv3.2-Speciale，这些模型要么缺乏深度推理的TIR支持，要么在Python TIR中表现不佳。值得注意的是，Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B 在 8 次尝试内在 LiveCodeBench Pro 硬分裂上达到了 0% 以上，在即使对人类来说也极其困难的问题上展现了强大的推理能力。120B-A12B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Qwen-3.5-122B-A10B. Notably, Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B achieves above 0% on the LiveCodeBench Pro hard split within 8 attempts, demonstrating strong reasoning ability on problems that are extremely difficult even for humans.

A. 基准和评估设置A. Benchmarks and Evaluation Setups

A.1.数学A.1. Math

A.1.1。非证明数学A.1.1. Non-proof Math

对于非证明数学推理任务，我们包括For non-proof math reasoning tasks, we include

  • 艾梅2025AIME 2025（MAA，2025）包含 2025 年美国数学邀请赛的 30 道题。(MAA, 2025) consists of 30 problems from American Invitational Mathematics Examination at 2025.

  • 2026年国际医疗器械展览会AIME 2026（MAA，2026）包含 2026 年美国数学邀请赛的 30 道题。(MAA, 2026) consists of 30 problems from American Invitational Mathematics Examination at 2026.

  • HMMT 2025 年 2 月HMMT Feb 2025（HMMT，2025）由哈佛-麻省理工学院数学锦标赛 2025 年 2 月数学竞赛的 30 个问题组成。(HMMT, 2025) consists of 30 problems from Harvard-MIT Mathematics Tournament 2025 February math competition.

  • IMO-AnswerBench（Luong 等人，2025）包含 400 个问题，其可验证的答案是从过去的奥林匹克竞赛中精心挑选的，然后由专家进行修改以避免记忆。(Luong et al., 2025) consists of 400 problems with verifiable answers carefully chosen from past Olympiad competitions and then altered by experts to avoid memorization.

对于在 AIME 2025、AIME 2026 和 HMMT 2025 Feb 上评估的 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3，我们将思维预算（最大响应长度）设置为 131K 个令牌，采样温度为 1.0，top-p 值为 1.0。对于 with-tool 设置，我们通过附加系统提示后缀来启用工具使用，允许模型调用有状态的 Python 执行器，最多可进行 100 次工具调用，最大响应长度为 131K 个令牌。对于 IMO-AnswerBench，我们设置为 256K 令牌，因为我们发现问题明显更加困难。我们使用并报告 LLM-Judge 分数，使用 GPT-OSS-120B（Agarwal 等人，2025）作为判断者，并使用 AnswerAutoGrader 提示（Luong 等人，2025）来确保 IMO-AnswerBench 上答案的正确性，因为简短的答案对于基于规则的验证器来说计算起来很复杂。继刘等人之后。 （2024 年、2026 年），我们报告 AIME/HMMT 的 avg@64 和 IMO-AnswerBench 的 avg@16。For Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3 evaluated on AIME 2025, AIME 2026 and HMMT 2025 Feb, we set the thinking budget (maximum response length) to 131K tokens, the sampling temperature to 1.0, the top-p value to 1.0. For the with-tool setting, we enable tool use by appending a system-prompt postfix, allowing the model to call a stateful Python executor for up to 100 tool calls with a maximum response length of 131K tokens. For IMO-AnswerBench, we set to 256K tokens because we found the questions are significantly more difficult. We use and report the LLM-Judge score using GPT-OSS-120B (Agarwal et al., 2025) as the judge and the AnswerAutoGrader prompt (Luong et al., 2025) for answer correctness on IMO-AnswerBench as the short answers are complicated for rule-based verifier to compute. Following Liu et al. (2024, 2026), we report avg@64 for AIME/HMMT and avg@16 for IMO-AnswerBench.

对于基线模型，我们使用其报告中的官方数据，或者如果官方数据不可用，则使用推荐的设置对其进行评估。For baseline models, we use official numbers from their reports or evaluate them with the recommended settings if the official numbers are unavailable.

A.1.2。数学证明A.1.2. Math Proof

对于数学证明任务，我们包括For math proof tasks, we include

  • 国际海事组织 2025IMO 2025（IMO，2025）由 IMO 2025 的 6 个问题组成。(IMO, 2025) consists of 6 problems from IMO 2025.

  • IMO-ProofBench（Luong et al., 2025）旨在评估人工智能模型构建全面且有效的数学论证的能力。该基准由 60 个基于证明的问题组成，旨在反映 IMO 中发现的问题类型。(Luong et al., 2025) is designed to evaluate the ability of AI models to construct comprehensive and valid mathematical arguments. This benchmark consists of 60 proof-based problems, curated to mirror the kinds of problems found in the IMO.

对于 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3，我们使用相同的指令，在 DeepSeek-Math-V2generate-verifyrefine 管道之后应用测试时间缩放。我们使用 NeMo-Skills 来实现这个管道（NVIDIA，2025）。我们使用 DeepSeek-Math-V2 中的默认超参数：128 个证明生成，每个证明 64 次验证，选择前 32 个证明进行细化，以及与每个证明配对的 8 次验证分析，优先考虑评分最低的分析。然后，我们生成 4 个精炼证明并持续最多 8 轮，或者直到平均证明分数达到 0.99999 的阈值。我们将最大生成长度设置为 256K 代币，温度为 1.0，top-p 为 0.95。For Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3, we apply test-time scaling following the DeepSeek-Math-V2 generate-verifyrefine pipeline, using the same instructions. We implement this pipeline with NeMo-Skills (NVIDIA, 2025). We use the default hyperparameters from DeepSeek-Math-V2: 128 proof generations, 64 verifications per proof, selection of the top 32 proofs for refinement, and 8 verification analyses paired with each proof, prioritizing the lowest-rated analyses. We then generate 4 refined proofs and continue for up to 8 rounds, or until the average proof score reaches the threshold of 0.99999. We set the maximum generation length to 256K tokens, with temperature 1.0 and top-p 0.95.

对于 IMO-ProofBench Basic 和 Advanced split 中的 11 个问题（即问题 1、4、7、13、14、17、19、22、25、26 和 28），我们将计算预算减少到 32 次证明生成、16 次验证、前 8 次证明和 2 轮以节省计算量。对于 IMO-ProofBench 评估，我们使用 DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale 确保结果稍后可重现，并在 ProofAutoGrader 提示下运行 64 次评分尝试（Luong 等人，2025）。我们发现 DeepSeek-Math-V2 在 Advanced 上的报告平均得分为 73.8For IMO-ProofBench Basic and 11 problems from the Advanced split (i.e., Problems 1, 4, 7, 13, 14, 17, 19, 22, 25, 26, and 28), we reduce the compute budget to 32 proof generations, 16 verifications, top 8 proofs, and 2 rounds to save compute. For IMO-ProofBench evaluation, we use DeepSeek-V3.2-Speciale to make sure the results are reproducible later and run 64 grading attempts with the ProofAutoGrader prompt (Luong et al., 2025). We found that reporting mean score yields 73.8 for DeepSeek-Math-V2 on the Advanced

20

Nemotron-Cascade 2：采用 Cascade RL 和多域按策略蒸馏的训练后法学硕士Nemotron-Cascade 2: Post-Training LLMs with Cascade RL and Multi-Domain On-Policy Distillation

分裂，这比人类的评分 61.9 慷慨得多。因此，我们在分析的基础上采用了一个简单的聚合规则：如果任何评委打出0分，则最终得分设置为0；否则，返回平均分。在此规则下，DeepSeek-Math-V2 获得 57.7，这更加接近人类评分，并将差异从 11.9 分减少到 4.2 分。split, which is substantially more generous than the human rating of 61.9. We therefore adopt a simple aggregation rule based on analysis: if any judge assigns a score of 0, the final score is set to 0; otherwise, return the mean score. Under this rule, DeepSeek-Math-V2 obtains 57.7, which is much closer to the human rating and reduces the discrepancy from 11.9 points to 4.2 points.

A.3.知识和 STEMA.3. Knowledge and STEM

对于知识推理任务，我们包括：For knowledge reasoning tasks, we include:

  • MMLU-Redux（Gema 等人，2024）是一个基准，由 30 个 MMLU 主题的 3,000 个手动重新注释问题的子集组成（Hendrycks 等人，2020），消除了原始注释错误。我们评估模型(Gema et al., 2024) is a benchmark consisting of a subset of 3,000 manually re-annotated questions across 30 MMLU subjects (Hendrycks et al., 2020), which eliminates the original annotation errors. We evaluate the models in思考thinking模式，并且由于测试集大小较大，因此根据每个问题的单代报告精确匹配 (EM) 准确性。mode and, due to the large test set size, report exact match (EM) accuracy based on a single generation per question.

  • MMLU-专业版MMLU-Pro（Wang 等人，2024）是原始 MMLU 基准的增强版本，通过扩展到超过 12,000 个研究生级别的问题并将答案选择从 4 个增加到 10 个，减轻了模型饱和度。我们报告的 EM 准确度为(Wang et al., 2024) is an enhanced version of the original MMLU benchmark that mitigates model saturation by expanding to over 12,000 graduate-level questions and increasing answer choices from four to ten. We report EM accuracy in思考thinking每个问题使用一代的模式。mode using one generation per question.

  • GPQA-钻石级GPQA-Diamond（Rein et al., 2024）是评估法学硕士科学推理能力的基准。它包含最高质量的 198 道 GPQA 问题，涵盖研究生水平的物理、生物和化学。我们报告 pass@1 准确率(Rein et al., 2024) is a benchmark for assessing an LLM’s scientific reasoning capability. It consists of the highest quality 198 GPQA questions covering graduate-level physics, biology, and chemistry. We report pass@1 accuracy in思考thinking模式，每个问题平均超过 8 代 (avg@8) 以减少方差。mode, averaged over 8 generations per question (avg@8) to reduce variance.

  • 哈尔滨HLE（Phan et al., 2025）是一个涵盖广泛专家级学科的前沿学术推理基准。我们对其纯文本拆分进行评估，其中包含 2,158 个示例。(Phan et al., 2025) is a frontier academic reasoning benchmark spanning a broad range of expert-level subjects. We evaluate on its text-only split, which contains 2,158 examples.

A.4.对齐和指令遵循A.4. Alignment and Instruction-Following

对于对齐任务，我们包括：For alignment tasks, we include:

  • 竞技场硬2.0ArenaHard 2.0(Li et al., 2024) 是一个人类偏好对齐基准，具有 750 个多样化且严格的真实用户提示。该数据集经过专门构建，包含 500 个针对开放式软件工程问题和复杂数学问题的提示，而其余 250 个则侧重于创意写作。它使用自动法学硕士作为法官的方法来估计人类相对于基线模型的偏好，从而实现全自动、低成本和快速评估，无需人工干预。在我们的实验中，我们报告的结果没有风格控制，以便与其他模型的官方报告数字进行直接比较。我们评估模型(Li et al., 2024) is a human-preference alignment benchmark featuring 750 diverse and rigorous real-user prompts. The dataset is specifically structured with 500 prompts targeting open-ended software engineering problems and complex mathematical questions, while the remaining 250 focus on creative writing. It uses an automatic LLM-as-Judge approach to estimate human preferences relative to a baseline model, enabling fully automated, low-cost, and fast evaluation without human intervention. In our experiments, we report results without style control to allow for straightforward comparison with the officially reported numbers of other models. We evaluate the models in思考thinking模式，并使用GPT-4.1作为自动判断。mode, and use GPT-4.1 as the automated judge.

It provides a separate constraint list to ensure no overlap between training and test constraints, enabling evaluation of an LLM’s generalization ability. The test set contains 294 prompts. We report pass@1 accuracy in思考thinking模式，平均超过 8 代 (avg@8)。mode, averaged over 8 generations (avg@8).

  • 规模化人工智能多重挑战Scale AI Multi-Challenge（Deshpande 等人，2025）是一个旨在评估法学硕士与人类用户进行多轮对话的基准。它由四个挑战类别组成：指令保留、推理记忆、可靠版本编辑和自我连贯性。这些任务需要模型同时执行准确的指令遵循、有效的上下文管理和上下文推理。测试集总共包含 273 个对话。我们报告了思维模式中 pass@1 的准确性，10 代人的平均值 (avg@10)。(Deshpande et al., 2025) is a benchmark designed to evaluate LLMs in multi-turn conversations with human users. It consists of four challenge categories: Instruction Retention, Inference Memory, Reliable Versioned Editing, and Self-Coherence. These tasks require models to simultaneously perform accurate instruction following, effective context management, and in-context reasoning. The test set contains 273 conversations in total. We report pass@1 accuracy in thinking mode, averaged across 10 generations (avg@10).

对于在 IFEval 上评估的 Nemotron-Cascade 模型For Nemotron-Cascade models evaluated on IFEval in不思考non-thinking模式，在 IFBench 和 ArenaHard 中mode, on IFBench and ArenaHard in思考thinking模式下，我们使用 0.6 的温度、0.95 的 top-p 值和 32K 令牌的最大响应长度。对于基线模型，我们会尽可能使用官方报告的结果；如果没有这样的结果，我们会使用他们推荐的推理配置或与我们相同的设置来评估它们。mode, we use a temperature of 0.6, a top-p value of 0.95, and a maximum response length of 32K tokens. A.5.长情境和情境学习A.5. Long Context and Context Learning

对于长上下文和上下文学习任务，我们包括：For long context and context learning tasks, we include:

  • AA-LCR（Team，2025）由 100 个具有挑战性的基于文本的问题组成，需要对多个长篇现实文档进行推理，包括公司报告、政府咨询、法律文件和学术论文。每个样本包含一个平均大约 100k 个标记的文档集。这些问题的设计使得答案不能直接从文档中检索，而是需要跨多个信息源进行推理。我们报告了思维模式中 pass@1 的准确性，16 代的平均值 (avg@16)。(Team, 2025) consists of 100 challenging text-based questions that require reasoning over multiple long, real-world documents, including company reports, government consultations, legal documents, and academic papers. Each sample contains a document set averaging approximately 100k tokens. The questions are designed such that answers cannot be directly retrieved from the documents and instead require reasoning across multiple sources of information. We report pass@1 accuracy in thinking mode, averaged over 16 generations (avg@16).

  • 长凳 v2LongBench v2(Bai et al., 2025) 包含 503 个具有挑战性的多项选择题，上下文长度从 8k 到 2M 单词不等。该基准涵盖六个任务类别：单文档 QA、多文档 QA、长上下文学习、长对话历史理解、代码存储库理解和长结构化数据理解。问题设计得很困难；即使配备文档搜索工具的人类专家也可能需要大量时间才能正确回答这些问题。我们以思维模式评估模型，并报告四代平均的 pass@1 准确度 (avg@4)。(Bai et al., 2025) contains 503 challenging multiple-choice questions with context lengths ranging from 8k to 2M words. The benchmark spans six task categories: single-document QA, multidocument QA, long in-context learning, long dialogue history understanding, code repository understanding, and long structured data understanding. The questions are designed to be difficult; even human experts equipped with document search tools may require substantial time to answer them correctly. We evaluate models in thinking mode and report pass@1 accuracy averaged over four generations (avg@4).

The benchmark contains 1,899 test samples spanning 500 complex contexts and 31,607 verification rubrics, all developed by experienced domain experts. The knowledge required to complete these tasks largely falls outside what existing models typically learn during pre-training, requiring models to learn directly from the provided context. Models are evaluated in thinking mode, and we report pass@1 accuracy from a single generation (avg@1).

Moving beyond simple code generation, this benchmark focuses on end-to-end workflows, requiring agents to demonstrate proficiency in holistic operations like model training, system configuration, and software debugging rather than just producing isolated functions. We evaluate the model using the default Terminus-2 scaffolding. We report avg@5 task success rate.

对于 SWE-bench Verified，我们使用 OpenHands 支架（Wang 等人，2025）作为代理编码评估框架。我们对代理轨迹采用完整的交互保留策略，保留工具调用、观察和模型输出的完整历史记录。这包括先前的文件视图、搜索结果、执行的命令和中间补丁，使模型能够在长期调试过程中有效地维护状态和推理。我们将最大上下文长度设置为 256K 令牌，并允许最多 200 个回合，这与我们基于执行的代理 SWE-RL 训练配置一致。值得注意的是，这种评估设置密切反映了我们的培训环境，因为两者都依赖于同一工具增强支架内基于执行的反馈和多轮交互。这种一致性减少了训练与测试的不匹配，并使模型能够更有效地将学习的行为（例如迭代调试、假设细化和工具驱动的推理）转移到评估设置。For SWE-bench Verified, we use the OpenHands scaffold (Wang et al., 2025) as the agentic coding evaluation framework. We adopt a full interaction retention policy for agent trajectories, preserving the complete history of tool calls, observations, and model outputs across turns. This includes prior file views, search results, executed commands, and intermediate patches, enabling the model to maintain state and reason effectively over long-horizon debugging processes. We set the maximum context length to 256K tokens and allow up to 200 turns, consistent with our execution-based agentic SWE-RL training configuration. Notably, this evaluation setup closely mirrors our training environment, as both rely on execution-based feedback and multi-turn interaction within the same tool-augmented scaffold. This alignment reduces train–test mismatch and enables the model to more effectively transfer learned behaviors, such as iterative debugging, hypothesis refinement, and tool-driven reasoning, to the evaluation setting.

C.2. HLE 法官提示C.2. HLE Judge Prompt

根据准确无歧义判断以下对[问题]的[回答]是否正确Judge whether the following [response] to [question] is correct or not based on the precise and unambiguous正确答案correct_answer下面。below.

[问题]：{问题} [回应]：{回应}[question]: {question} [response]: {response}

您的判断必须采用以下指定的格式和标准：Your judgement must be in the format and criteria specified below:

extract_final_answer：从 [response] 中提取的最终准确答案。如果没有从响应中提取准确的最终答案，则将提取的答案设置为“无”。extracted_final_answer: The final exact answer extracted from the [response]. Put the extracted answer as ’None’ if there is no exact, final answer to extract from the response.

推理：解释为什么 extract_final_answer 是正确或不正确的reasoning: Explain why the extracted_final_answer is correct or incorrect based on正确答案correct_answer，仅关注之间是否存在有意义的差异, focusing only on if there are meaningful differences between正确答案correct_answer和提取的最终答案。不要评论问题的任何背景，不要试图解决问题，不要争论任何与其他问题不同的答案and the extracted_final_answer. Do not comment on any background to the problem, do not attempt to solve the problem, do not argue for any answer different than正确答案correct_answer，仅关注答案是否匹配。, focus only on whether the answers match.

正确：如果 extract_final_answer 与correct: Answer ’yes’ if extracted_final_answer matches the正确答案correct_answer上面给出的，或者对于数值问题来说在很小的误差范围内。否则回答“否”，即如果存在任何不一致、歧义、不等价，或者提取的答案不正确。given above, or is within a small margin of error for numerical problems. Answer ’no’ otherwise, i.e. if there if there is any inconsistency, ambiguity, non-equivalency, or if the extracted answer is incorrect.

置信度：提取的置信度得分在 0|%| 之间和 100|%|来自[回复]。如果没有可用的置信度分数，则输入 100。confidence: The extracted confidence score between 0|%| and 100|%| from [response]. Put 100 if there is no confidence score available.

D. ELO 评级分析D. ELO Rating Analysis

我们基于 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B 模型进行 ELO 评级分析We perform ELO rating analysis on our Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B model based on40最近在 2501 年至 2507 年期间举行的 Div.1 和 Div.2 Codeforces 竞赛。问题和评估由 LiveCodeBench Pro 提供（Zheng et al., 2025）。我们采用与 Wang 等人类似的评级估计方法。 （2025），通过允许模型达到recent Div.1 and Div.2 Codeforces contests held between 2501–2507. Problems and evaluations are provided by LiveCodeBench Pro (Zheng et al., 2025). We adopt similar rating estimation approach as in Wang et al. (2025), by allowing model with up to𝑁= 每个竞赛问题 8 个提交，评估模型性能以及与人类参赛者的相对排名，并考虑预期的惩罚。我们使用 1.0 的温度、0.95 的 top-p 和 128K 的最大代币预算生成模型的响应。我们的 Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B 模型的性能详细信息（= 8 submissions to each contest problems, estimating model performance and relative ranking to human contestants with expected penalty consideration. We generate the model’s responses using a temperature of 1.0, top-p of 0.95, and a maximum token budget of 128K. The performance details of our Nemotron-Cascade-2-30B-A3B model (与with和and没有withoutpython-tool use）分别见表11和表12。python-tool use) can be found in Table 11 and Table 12, respectively.

我们观察到我们的模型具有强大的代码推理能力，可以解决真正棘手的问题，甚至在某些 Div 上也能获得高排名。 1轮（999轮、1012轮、1015轮、1021轮等），同时在解决简单到中等水平的问题上保持稳定的表现。然而，这些模型在处理需要建设性算法、交互方式和假设驱动思想的问题方面仍然存在弱点。We observed our model’s strong code reasoning ability on solving really tough problems and achieving high ranking even on some Div. 1 rounds (Round 999, 1012, 1015, 1021 etc.), while maintaining stable performance on solving easy-medium level problems. However, the models still has weakness on dealing with problems that requiring constructive algorithms, interactive manner, and hypothesis-driven ideas.

问题4Problem 4

正整数的真因数A proper divisor of a positive integer𝑁是一个正除数is a positive divisor of𝑁除了other than𝑁本身。无限序列itself. The infinite sequence𝑎 1 , 𝑎 2 ,···, · · ·由正整数组成，每个正整数至少有三个真因数。对于每个consists of positive integers, each of which has at least three proper divisors. 问题5Problem 5

Alice 和 Bazza 正在玩 inekoalaty 游戏，这是一种两人游戏，其规则取决于正实数Alice and Bazza are playing the inekoalaty game, a two-player game whose rules depend on a positive real number𝜆这是双方玩家都知道的。上which is known to both players. On the𝑛游戏的第回合（从th turn of the game (starting with𝑛= 1) 发生以下情况：= 1) the following happens:

  • If 𝑛是奇数，Alice 选择一个非负实数is odd, Alice chooses a nonnegative real number𝑥𝑛这样such that

𝑥1+1 +𝑥2+2 +···· · · + 𝑥𝑛 ≤ 𝜆𝑛。𝑥𝑛 ≤ 𝜆𝑛.

• 如果• If𝑛是偶数，Bazza 选择一个非负实数is even, Bazza chooses a nonnegative real number𝑥𝑛这样such that𝑥[2] 1[+] [𝑥][ 𝑥][2] 2[+] [···][ · · ·][+][ +][𝑥][ 𝑥][2] 𝑛[≤][𝑛.]

如果玩家无法选择合适的If a player cannot choose a suitable𝑥𝑛，游戏结束，另一位玩家获胜。如果游戏永远持续下去，则双方都不会获胜。所有选择的号码都是双方玩家都知道的。, the game ends and the other player wins. If the game goes on forever, neither player wins. All chosen numbers are known to both players.

From (8),𝑑𝑘 ≥ (2 𝜆 -𝜆 − ~~√~~ 2) 𝑘 + 𝜆>全部为 00 for all𝑘（因为右侧为正(since the right side is positive for𝑘= 1 因为 3= 1 because 3𝜆 -𝜆 − ~~√~~2~~ 2>0 为0 for𝜆> ~~√~~2~~ 2/2）。因此，当 Alice 还不想赢时，她总是可以选择 0。因此，她的策略非常明确，可以保证在有限的时间内获胜。2). Hence Alice can always pick 0 when she does not want to win yet. Thus her strategy is well defined and guarantees a win in finite time.结论为Conclusion for 𝜆> ~~√~~2~~ 2/ 2 :爱丽丝有一个获胜策略。Alice has a winning strategy.

最终答案Final answer

• 爱丽丝有一个制胜策略• Alice has a winning strategy当且仅当iff 𝜆> ~~√~~ 2 2[.]

• 巴扎有制胜策略• Bazza has a winning strategy当且仅当iff 𝜆< ~~√~~ 2 2[.]
